data-id="9b45696" data-element_type="widget" data-widget_type="text-editor.default"> <div class="elementor-widget-container"> <p><span style="font-weight: 400;">We use a <strong>molecular approach combining hundreds of Single Nucleotide Polymorphisms (SNPs) and Short Tandem Repeat (STR) in the genome</strong> to analyze non-invasive chimpanzee samples, identifying individuals, their species or subspecies, and genetic relationships. This ethical, low-impact approach is ideal for monitoring primate populations without disturbing their natural behavior.</span></p><p><span style="font-weight: 400;">Supported by our academic expertise in primate genomics, and thanks to our extended genomic database, our method ensures highly accurate and reproducible results in a substantial fraction of the collected samples. </span><span style="font-weight: 400;">Our innovative approach is a powerful tool that combines scientific precision with real-world applicability for conservation and environmental assessment.</span></p> </div> elementor-widget-text-editor" data-id="46fe557" data-element_type="widget" data-widget_type="text-editor.default"> <div class="elementor-widget-container"> <p><span style="font-weight: 400;">Our methods are based on SNP-Amplicon sequencing, is a high-throughput genotyping platform designed to simultaneously analyze hundreds of genetic markers (SNPs) and STR genotyping  across thousands of samples. The method has been developed for large-scale population genetics studies to providing a technology for a cost-effective genotyping. This method is particularly suitable for projects relying on non-invasive samples such as feces and hairs, and it offers unique advantages over</span><span style="font-weight: 400;">existing methods.</span></p> </div> </div> </div> </div> </div> </details> <details id="e-n-accordion-item-1961" class="e-n-accordion-item" > <summary class="e-n-accordion-item-title" data-accordion-index="2" elementor-widget-text-editor" data-id="e9bd473" data-element_type="widget" data-widget_type="text-editor.default"> <div class="elementor-widget-container"> <p><span style="font-weight: 400;">Conservation monitoring requires tools that are robust, affordable, and applicable to degraded DNA. Our method addresses these needs more effectively than current alternatives such as RAD-seq, microsatellites, genomic capture or commercial SNP arrays among others. </span></p><ul><li>Cost-efficiency and scalability</li><li><span style="font-weight: 400;">Suitability for degraded DNA</span></li><li><span style="font-weight: 400;">High accuracy and reproducibility              </span></li><li><span style="font-weight: 400;">Still sample dependent. </span><span style="font-weight: 400;">Our method can not recover genotypes from samples with ultra low DNA content or contaminated from diet or external manipulation</span></li></ul> </div> </div> </div> </div> </div> </details> <details id="e-n-accordion-item-1962" class="e-n-accordion-item" > <summary class="e-n-accordion-item-title" genetic approach can provide conservationists, regulators, and industry partners with an unprecedented ability to track individuals and, assess population structure and
